## Output

```
**Position**
1. **Fix classifier.** Shipping yogurt with known understated grades is not defensible. Acknowledge-only is for unavoidable uncertainty, not a confirmed parser inversion that turns a stabilizer into a benign starch bucket.

2. **Split is sound with a deployment guard.** Apply the engine fix and rescore pre-launch yogurt now. Queue hummus/cakes/crackers JSON regeneration and redeploy behind explicit owner approval. If the live site/API calculates from the engine at runtime, owner approval is required before deploying the engine fix itself.

3. **Regex needs tightening.** `(?:\s+\S+){1,2}` risks crossing punctuation/ingredient boundaries. The matcher must be bounded to the same ingredient phrase and preserve `לא מעובד`. Also scan taxonomy for other source-qualified variants where a source word can split the decisive additive phrase, especially pectin/gelatin/lecithin/starch-style entries.

4. **Correction is proportionate.** The crossings are evidence the classifier mattered, not that ECS −3 is wrong. Do not reopen ECS weight inside this bug fix. If modified starch severity is disputed, make it a separate scoring-governance proposal with evidence, observability, scope, and rollback.

**Recommendation**
**fix-now-split**: correct the classifier and rescore non-published yogurt immediately; hold all live-category JSON regeneration/redeploy for owner approval because published scores will move.

**Required Guardrail**
Before merge/deploy: full cross-corpus baseline diff, phrase-boundary-safe matcher tests including native `לא מעובד`, and explicit proof that no live page/API reflows without owner-gated regenerated assets.
